| Objective | Journal path |
| --- | --- |
| A clandestine meeting of reptilians at midnight? V, we're not missing this. If you want answers to the questions plaguing humankind for eons, this might be your only shot. Is there life in the cosmos? Is there anyone pulling the strings? If so, why? And why the fuck do they let all this evil shit happen? And to think the Universe's greatest secrets could be found in some old factory in our humble little Night City... (Primary) | `quests/minor_quest/mq026_conspiracy/stash/01_01_find_stash` |

| `realtime_delay` | delamain_holocall.questphase / h0 / 80; delamain_holocall.questphase / h0 / 81; delamain_holocall.questphase / h899 / 3; delamain_holocall.scene / scene / 1020; +273 locations in catalogue.json | real-time duration + cancellation owner → elapsed signal. Do not substitute game time for real time; retire delayed effects after cancellation. |

Trace every CutSource destination; cancellation must not commit success. |

Validate every consumed entry/exit and distinguish terminating from notification outputs. |
| `target_resolution` | mq026.questphase / h0 / 79; mq026.questphase / h142 / 16; mq026_01_prophecy.scene / scene / 1022; mq026_03_nomad.scene / scene / 110; +7 locations in catalogue.json | target set + lethal/unconscious/defeated policy → target resolved. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../expanded-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.
